# Labels of the LF Note and the Constants Table

Each content line of WILSON_IT1_LARGE_FIELD_DEPTH_2026_10_27.md (LF) and of CONSTANTS_AND_CONVENTIONS_WDELTA.md (table) carries a fixed tag at its start, {#s.n} in the LF and {#Ts.n} in the table: s the section (P the preamble, 0 the Open Gaps, A the appendix of the LF), n the position of the line in its section when the tags were added. Headings carry {#Ss} and {#TSs} at their end. Tags are never renumbered; a new line gets a new tag. When the tags were added no line was added, removed, or split; lines have been added since, so an old pointer l.N holds only at the revision it names, and this file gives each tag's current line.

Named steps inside long lines carry semantic tags at their start (below). A reference reads, for example, LF #K0.f or table {#T11.3}; the LF and the notes that cite it cite its lines and paragraphs by tag (#4.521, #A.11.25), never by line number, so lines may be split, added, or moved.
